    e.target.style.background = 'transparent';

  }, 'Try GitHub Copilot free')
);
5. DECORATIVE CLOUDS
javascript// Soft floating clouds for depth
const decorativeCloud = (x, y, size = 200, opacity = 0.6) => h('div', { s: `
  position: absolute;
  width: ${size}px;
  height: ${size * 0.6}px;
  left: ${x}%;
  top: ${y}px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, 
    rgba(139, 92, 246, ${opacity * 0.3}), 
    rgba(167, 139, 250, ${opacity * 0.2}));
  border-radius: 50%;
  filter: blur(40px);
  pointer-events: none;
  animation: float ${8 + Math.random() * 4}s ease-in-out infinite;
` });

const floatStyle = `
  @keyframes float {
    0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0px) translateX(0px); }
    50% { transform: translateY(-20px) translateX(10px); }
  }
`;

I want this hard wired into our Blue Print DNA.So please write the "Bible" so to speak, to instruct all future operations on how to make quality lessonsI am sharing my other chats latest share for more reference📘 NET LESSON BLUEPRINT BIBLE v1.0
The Complete Gospel for Natural English Training Lesson Design

🎯 MISSION STATEMENT
Every NET lesson must empower students to learn English naturally through:

Engaging storytelling that captures attention
Interactive games that build confidence
Beautiful design that inspires learning
Measurable progress that motivates growth


🎨 SECTION 1: AESTHETIC GOSPEL (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
1.1 Color Palette (MANDATORY)
css/* PRIMARY COLORS - Use these EVERYWHERE */
--neon-purple: #a855f7;    /* Primary brand color */
--deep-purple: #7e22ce;    /* Darker accent */
--glow-purple: #c084fc;    /* Lighter highlights */
--gold: #f59e0b;           /* Secondary brand color */
--deep-gold: #d97706;      /* Darker gold */
--glow-gold: #fbbf24;      /* Lighter gold */

/* BACKGROUNDS */
--dark: #0f0f1a;           /* Primary background */
--darker: #0a0a0f;         /* Deepest background */
--dark-tint: #0d0a14;      /* Purple-tinted variant */

/* NEUTRALS */
--border: #27273a;         /* Subtle borders */
--text: #e5e7eb;           /* Primary text */
--text-dim: #9ca3af;       /* Secondary text */
--text-muted: #71717a;     /* Tertiary text */
❌ FORBIDDEN COLORS:

Blue (#38bdf8, #3b82f6) - Wrong brand
Red (except for errors)
Green (except for success states)
Any color NOT listed above


1.2 Gradients (REQUIRED PATTERNS)
css/* Hero Titles */
background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--glow-purple), var(--gold));
-webkit-background-clip: text;
-webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;

/* Buttons */
background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--deep-purple));

/* Cards */
background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(26,26,38,0.8), rgba(15,15,26,0.8));

/* Progress Bars */
background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--gold));

/* Top Border Accent */
background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--gold), var(--neon-purple));

1.3 Glow Effects (ALWAYS INCLUDE)
css/* Text Glow */
text-shadow: 0 0 20px var(--gold);

/* Box Glow */
box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168,85,247,0.3);

/* Hover Glow (buttons/cards) */
box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(168,85,247,0.5);

/* Border Glow */
border: 2px solid var(--neon-purple);
box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(168,85,247,0.3);

1.4 Glassmorphism (ALL CARDS)
css/* Standard Card */
background: rgba(26,26,38,0.7);
backdrop-filter: blur(20px) saturate(180%);
border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
border-radius: 1rem;
box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);

/* Elevated Card */
background: rgba(34,34,58,0.6);
backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,0.1);

1.5 Organic Blobs (BACKGROUND DEPTH)
css/* Floating gradient spheres */
.blob {
  position: fixed;
  width: 600px;
  height: 600px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(168,85,247,0.3), transparent);
  border-radius: 40% 60% 70% 30% / 40% 50% 60% 50%;
  filter: blur(80px);
  pointer-events: none;
  animation: morph 15s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes morph {
  0%, 100% { border-radius: 40% 60% 70% 30% / 40% 50% 60% 50%; }
  50% { border-radius: 60% 40% 30% 70% / 50% 60% 40% 50%; }
}
Placement:

Top-left: Purple blob
Bottom-right: Gold blob
Always position: fixed with z-index: 0


1.6 Typography Standards
css/* Hero Titles */
font-size: clamp(2rem, 6vw, 4rem);
font-weight: 800;
line-height: 1.1;
letter-spacing: -0.02em;

/* Section Titles */

/* Body Text */
font-size: 1rem;
line-height: 1.6;
color: var(--text);

/* Small Text */

color: var(--text-dim);
Fonts:

Primary: System stack (-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif)
Code/Tech: 'JetBrains Mono', Monaco, monospace (only for technical elements)

html<!-- Hero Section -->

Comprehension Quiz10 min3-5 questions, instant feedback5. Game 1: Wheel of Names10 minRandom student selector6. Game 2: Interactive15 minReflex/Speed/Memory game7. Game 3: Strategy15 minMatching/Puzzle/Team challenge8. Feedback & Export5 minStar rating + JSON download

2.2 Hero Section Template
html<div class="hero">
  <div style="font-size: 4rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">⚡</div>
  <h1 class="hero-title">[Lesson Title]</h1>
  <p class="hero-subtitle">A 90-Minute Natural English Training Lesson</p>
  
  <!-- Progress Bar -->
  <div class="progress">
    <div class="progress-fill" id="overall-progress" style="width: 0%"></div>
  </div>

</div>
Hero Rules:

Must include progress tracking
Must show NET branding
Must use gradient title
Must have glassmorphism background


2.3 Vocabulary Builder Template
html<div class="vocab-grid">
  <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('word')">
    <div class="vocab-word">Word</div>
    <div class="vocab-def">Definition here</div>
    <div style="color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
  </div>
</div>
Vocabulary Rules:

8-10 words MAXIMUM
Each word must have:

Bold word in gold (#f59e0b)
Simple definition (1 sentence)
Audio playback via Web Speech API
Hover glow effect


Grid layout: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))

Audio Implementation:
javascriptfunction playAudio(word) {
  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(word);
  utterance.lang = 'en-US';
  speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
}

2.4 Reading Section Template
html<div class="reading">
  <p><strong>Part 1: Title</strong></p>
  <p>First paragraph with <strong>key vocabulary</strong> highlighted.</p>
  
  <p><strong>Part 2: Title</strong></p>
  <p>Second paragraph continuing the story...</p>
  
  <!-- 3-5 parts total -->
</div>
Reading Rules:

3-5 parts with clear subheadings
600-1000 words total
Highlight key vocabulary in bold + gold color
Left border accent in gold
Line height: 1.8 for readability
Font size: 1.1rem minimum

Style:
css.reading {
  background: rgba(15,15,26,0.6);
  border-left: 4px solid var(--gold);
  padding: 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 0.5rem;
  line-height: 1.8;
}
.reading strong {
  color: var(--gold);
  text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--gold);
}

2.5 Quiz Template
html<div style="margin: 2rem 0;">
  <p style="font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 1rem;">1. Question text?</p>
  <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, true, 1)">Correct answer</div>
  <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 1)">Wrong answer</div>
  <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 1)">Wrong answer</div>
</div>
Quiz Rules:

3-5 questions ONLY (don't overload)
Each question: 3 options
Click once to answer (lock after selection)
Visual feedback:

Correct: Green background (#22c55e)
Wrong: Red background (#ef4444)


Track score in real-time
Display final score: X/Y Correct

JavaScript:
javascriptlet quizScore = 0;
function answerQuiz(el, correct, qNum) {
  const siblings = el.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.quiz-option');
  siblings.forEach(s => s.style.pointerEvents = 'none');
  el.classList.add(correct ? 'quiz-correct' : 'quiz-wrong');
  if (correct) quizScore++;
  document.getElementById('quiz-score').textContent = quizScore + '/3';
}

2.6 Game 1: Wheel of Names
Purpose: Randomly select students for speaking practice
Template:
html<div class="game-area">
  <div class="wheel" id="wheel">Press SPIN</div>
  <button class="btn" onclick="spinWheel()">🔥 SPIN</button>
  <div id="picked-student"></div>
</div>
JavaScript:
javascriptconst students = ['Student 1', 'Student 2', /* ... */];
let wheelInterval = null;
let wheelIndex = 0;

function spinWheel() {
  if (wheelInterval) return;
  wheelInterval = setInterval(() => {
    wheelIndex = (wheelIndex + 1) % students.length;
    document.getElementById('wheel').textContent = students[wheelIndex];
  }, 80);
  
  setTimeout(() => {
    clearInterval(wheelInterval);
    wheelInterval = null;
    document.getElementById('picked-student').textContent = 
      '✨ ' + students[wheelIndex] + ' ✨';
  }, 2000 + Math.random() * 2000);
}
Wheel Rules:

Student list MUST be configurable
Animation: 80ms interval
Auto-stop after 2-4 seconds
Display picked student in gold with stars
Allow manual stop button


2.7 Game 2: Reflex Test
Purpose: Test vocabulary recognition speed
Template:
html<div class="game-area">
  <div id="reflex-word" style="font-size: 3rem; font-weight: 800;">Ready?</div>
  <button class="btn" onclick="startReflexGame()">Start Test</button>
  <div class="timer" id="reflex-time">0 ms</div>
  <div class="score-value" id="reflex-score">0/5</div>
</div>
Game Flow:

Show "Get Ready..." (gray)
Wait 1.5-3.5 seconds (random)
Show vocabulary word in GREEN
Measure click time
Display reaction time in milliseconds
Repeat 5 times
Show average speed

Reflex Rules:

Use vocabulary words from lesson
5 rounds maximum
Green text = clickable
Track best time and average
Reward < 300ms as "Lightning Fast!"


2.8 Game 3: Memory Match
Purpose: Reinforce vocabulary retention
Template:
html<div id="memory-grid" style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 1rem;"></div>
Game Mechanics:

4 pairs (8 cards total)
Match word with definition
Flip 2 cards at a time
Green border when matched
Reset if no match
Track pairs found

Memory Rules:

Use 4 words from vocabulary
Cards start face-down showing "?"
Click to flip
Max 2 flipped at once
Auto-reset wrong pairs after 800ms
Celebrate completion


2.9 Feedback & Export
html<div class="feedback-stars" id="stars">
  <span onclick="rate(1)">⭐</span>
  <span onclick="rate(2)">⭐</span>
  <span onclick="rate(3)">⭐</span>
  <span onclick="rate(4)">⭐</span>
  <span onclick="rate(5)">⭐</span>
</div>

<textarea id="comment" placeholder="Comments?"></textarea>
<button class="btn" onclick="exportFeedback()">📥 Export JSON</button>
Export JSON Structure:
json{
  "lesson": "Lesson Title",
  "timestamp": "2025-01-01T12:00:00Z",
  "vocab": ["word1", "word2"],
  "quiz": [
    {"question": 1, "correct": true},
    {"question": 2, "correct": false}
  ],
  "games": {
    "wheel": ["Student Name"],
    "reflex": 4,
    "memory": 4
  },
  "rating": 5,
  "comment": "Great lesson!"
}

**FAIL if < 16/20**

---

### **4.4 User Experience (10 points)**

| Criteria | Points | Pass Threshold |
|----------|--------|----------------|
| Progress tracking | 3 | Live progress bar |
| Score displays | 3 | Real-time feedback |
| Smooth transitions | 2 | CSS animations present |
| Mobile responsive | 2 | Works on tablets/phones |

**FAIL if < 8/10**

Mascot Behaviors:
javascriptconst Sparky = {
  greet: () => showSpeech("Welcome! Let's start learning!"),
  encourage: () => showSpeech("Great job! Keep going!"),
  celebrate: () => {
    showSpeech("🎉 Amazing work!");
    animateMascot('bounce');
  },
  hint: (text) => showSpeech(`💡 Hint: ${text}`),
  error: () => showSpeech("Oops! Try again!")
};

function animateMascot(type) {
  const mascot = document.getElementById('sparky');
  mascot.classList.add(`mascot-${type}`);
  setTimeout(() => mascot.classList.remove(`mascot-${type}`), 1000);
}
Mascot CSS:
css.mascot {
  position: fixed;
  bottom: 2rem;
  right: 2rem;
  z-index: 1000;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.mascot-body {
  font-size: 4rem;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 20px var(--neon-purple));
  animation: float 3s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes float {
  0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); }
  50% { transform: translateY(-20px); }
}

.mascot-bounce {
  animation: bounce 0.5s ease-out !important;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); }
  50% { transform: translateY(-40px); }
}

.mascot-speech {
  background: rgba(168,85,247,0.9);
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 1rem;
  color: white;
  font-weight: 600;
  margin-top: 1rem;
  box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(168,85,247,0.5);
  opacity: 0;
  transition: opacity 0.3s;
}

.mascot-speech.active {

}

1.3 Advanced Micro-Interactions
css/* Particle Effects on Click */
.particle-burst {
  position: absolute;
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  background: var(--gold);
  border-radius: 50%;
  animation: burst 0.6s ease-out forwards;
}

@keyframes burst {
  from {
    transform: translate(0, 0) scale(1);
    opacity: 1;
  }
  to {
    transform: translate(var(--x), var(--y)) scale(0);
    opacity: 0;
  }
}

/* Success Ripple */
.success-ripple {
  position: absolute;
  border: 3px solid var(--success);
  border-radius: 50%;
  animation: ripple 1s ease-out;
}

@keyframes ripple {
  from {
    width: 0;
    height: 0;
    opacity: 1;
  }
  to {
    width: 200px;
    height: 200px;
    opacity: 0;
  }
}
Implementation:
javascriptfunction createParticleBurst(x, y, color = 'var(--gold)') {
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    const particle = document.createElement('div');
    particle.className = 'particle-burst';
    particle.style.left = x + 'px';
    particle.style.top = y + 'px';
    particle.style.background = color;
    particle.style.setProperty('--x', Math.cos(i * 30 * Math.PI / 180) * 100 + 'px');
    particle.style.setProperty('--y', Math.sin(i * 30 * Math.PI / 180) * 100 + 'px');
    document.body.appendChild(particle);
    setTimeout(() => particle.remove(), 600);
  }
}

2.2 Pre-Assessment (NEW)
html<div class="card" id="section-assessment">
  <h2 class="card-title"><span>🎯</span> Quick Skill Check</h2>
  <p>Let's find the right difficulty for you!</p>
  
  <div class="assessment-question">
    <p><strong>How confident are you with English vocabulary?</strong></p>
    <div class="confidence-slider">
      <input type="range" min="1" max="10" value="5" id="vocab-confidence">
      <div class="slider-labels">
        <span>Beginner</span>
        <span>Expert</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  
  <div class="assessment-question">
    <p><strong>Read this sentence and tell us if you understand:</strong></p>
    <p class="sample-text">"The shepherd realized that his trick had serious consequences."</p>
    <button class="btn" onclick="assessReading(true)">I understand ✓</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="assessReading(false)">Need help ✗</button>
  </div>
  
  <button class="btn" onclick="startLesson()">Start Lesson →</button>
</div>
Adaptive Logic:
javascriptconst assessment = {
  vocabConfidence: 5,
  readingLevel: null,
  recommendedDifficulty: 'medium'
};

function assessReading(understood) {
  assessment.readingLevel = understood ? 'advanced' : 'basic';
  calculateDifficulty();
}

function calculateDifficulty() {
  const vocab = assessment.vocabConfidence;
  const reading = assessment.readingLevel === 'advanced' ? 8 : 4;
  const avg = (vocab + reading) / 2;
  
  if (avg >= 8) assessment.recommendedDifficulty = 'hard';
  else if (avg >= 5) assessment.recommendedDifficulty = 'medium';
  else assessment.recommendedDifficulty = 'easy';
  
  Sparky.greet();
  adjustLessonDifficulty(assessment.recommendedDifficulty);
}

function adjustLessonDifficulty(level) {
  // Adjust vocab count, reading length, quiz complexity
  const settings = {
    easy: { vocabCount: 6, readingParts: 3, quizCount: 3 },
    medium: { vocabCount: 8, readingParts: 5, quizCount: 4 },
    hard: { vocabCount: 10, readingParts: 7, quizCount: 5 }
  };
  
  Object.assign(lessonSettings, settings[level]);
  document.getElementById('difficulty-badge').textContent = level.toUpperCase();
  document.getElementById('difficulty-badge').style.background = 
    level === 'easy' ? 'var(--easy)' : 
    level === 'medium' ? 'var(--medium)' : 
    'var(--hard)';
}

html<div class="vocab-card enhanced">

      🎤 Record
    </button>
    <div class="pronunciation-feedback" id="feedback-shepherd"></div>
  </div>

Voice Recording Implementation:
javascriptlet mediaRecorder = null;
let audioChunks = [];

async function recordPronunciation(word) {
  const btn = document.getElementById(`record-${word}`);
  
  if (!mediaRecorder || mediaRecorder.state === 'inactive') {
    // Start recording
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
    mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
    
    mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (e) => audioChunks.push(e.data);
    mediaRecorder.onstop = () => analyzePronunciation(word);
    
    mediaRecorder.start();
    btn.textContent = '⏸️ Stop';
    btn.style.background = 'var(--error)';
    
    // Auto-stop after 3 seconds
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (mediaRecorder.state === 'recording') {
        mediaRecorder.stop();
      }
    }, 3000);
  } else {
    // Stop recording
    mediaRecorder.stop();
    btn.textContent = '🎤 Record';
    btn.style.background = '';
  }
}

async function analyzePronunciation(word) {
  const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunks, { type: 'audio/webm' });
  audioChunks = [];
  
  // v2.0: Send to AI for pronunciation analysis
  const feedback = await sendToAI(audioBlob, word);
  
  const feedbackEl = document.getElementById(`feedback-${word}`);
  feedbackEl.innerHTML = `
    <div class="pronunciation-score">
      <div class="score-circle" style="background: conic-gradient(var(--${feedback.grade >= 80 ? 'success' : 'warning'}) ${feedback.grade}%, var(--dark) 0);">
        <span>${feedback.grade}%</span>
      </div>
      <div class="score-text">${feedback.message}</div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  if (feedback.grade >= 80) {
    Sparky.celebrate();
    createParticleBurst(feedbackEl.offsetLeft, feedbackEl.offsetTop);
  } else {
    Sparky.hint(feedback.tip);
  }
}

// Mock AI function (replace with actual API)
async function sendToAI(audioBlob, targetWord) {
  // Simulate AI processing
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000));
  
  const score = Math.floor(Math.random() * 30) + 70;
  return {
    grade: score,
    message: score >= 80 ? 'Excellent pronunciation!' : 'Good try! Focus on the vowel sound.',
    tip: score < 80 ? 'Try emphasizing the first syllable' : ''
  };
}

Annotation System:
css.annotate {
  color: var(--gold);
  border-bottom: 2px dotted var(--gold);
  cursor: help;
  position: relative;
}

.annotate::after {
  content: attr(data-definition);
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 100%;
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(-10px);
  background: rgba(168,85,247,0.95);
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 1rem;
  border-radius: 0.5rem;
  font-size: 0.875rem;
  white-space: nowrap;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: opacity 0.3s, transform 0.3s;
  z-index: 100;
  box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168,85,247,0.5);
}

.annotate:hover::after {
  opacity: 1;
  transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
}

javascriptasync function recordSpeaking(promptId) {
  const feedback = await analyzeSpeaking(promptId);
  
  const feedbackEl = document.getElementById(`speaking-feedback-${promptId}`);

        </div>
        <div class="score-item">

        </div>
        <div class="score-item">
          <span>Confidence</span>
          <div class="score-bar">
            <div class="score-fill" style="width: ${feedback.confidence}%; background: var(--success);"></div>
          </div>
          <span>${feedback.confidence}%</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="overall-speaking-score">
        Overall: <strong>${feedback.overall}%</strong>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  if (feedback.overall >= 80) {
    Sparky.celebrate();
  } else {
    Sparky.encourage();
  }
}

Persistent Leaderboard:
javascriptconst Leaderboard = {
  data: JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('net_leaderboard') || '{"1":0,"2":0,"3":0,"4":0}'),
  
  addPoints(team, points) {
    this.data[team] = (this.data[team] || 0) + points;
    this.save();
    this.render();
    this.celebrate(team, points);
  },
  
  save() {
    localStorage.setItem('net_leaderboard', JSON.stringify(this.data));
  },
  
  render() {
    const grid = document.getElementById('leaderboard-grid');
    const sorted = Object.entries(this.data).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
    
    grid.innerHTML = sorted.map(([team, score], index) => `
      <div class="leaderboard-row rank-${index + 1}">
        <div class="rank">${index + 1}</div>
        <div class="team-info">
          <span class="team-icon">${['🔴','🔵','🟢','🟡'][team - 1]}</span>
          <span class="team-name">Team ${team}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="team-score">${score} ⭐</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
  },
  
  celebrate(team, points) {
    const notification = document.createElement('div');
    notification.className = 'team-notification';
    notification.style.cssText = `
      position: fixed;
      top: 2rem;
      right: 2rem;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--team-${team}), var(--gold));
      color: white;
      padding: 1rem 2rem;
      border-radius: 1rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      box-shadow: 0 0 40px var(--team-${team});
      z-index: 9999;
      animation: slideIn 0.5s ease-out;
    `;
    notification.textContent = `Team ${team} earned ${points} stars! ⭐`;
    document.body.appendChild(notification);
    setTimeout(() => notification.remove(), 3000);
  }
};

Video API Control:
javascriptlet player;

function onYouTubeIframeAPIReady() {
  player = new YT.Player('lesson-video', {
    events: {
      'onReady': onPlayerReady,
      'onStateChange': onPlayerStateChange
    }
  });
}

function controlVideo(action) {
  if (!player) return;
  if (action === 'play') player.playVideo();
  else if (action === 'pause') player.pauseVideo();
}

function skipTo(seconds) {
  if (!player) return;
  player.seekTo(seconds, true);
}

function onPlayerStateChange(event) {
  if (event.data === YT.PlayerState.ENDED) {
    Sparky.celebrate();
    Leaderboard.addPoints(currentTeam, 5);
  }
}
Video Requirements v2.0:

Max 5 minutes per video
Must have closed captions
Include 2-3 comprehension questions
Auto-pause at key moments for discussion
Award points for completion

    </div>
    
    <div class="team-timer">
      <div class="timer-display">
        <span id="challenge-timer">3:00</span>
      </div>
      <button class="btn" onclick="startChallenge()">Start Challenge</button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
Challenge Logic:
javascriptconst Challenge = {
  sentence: "Lying destroys trust and has serious consequences".split(' '),
  timer: 180, // 3 minutes
  interval: null,
  
  start() {
    const scrambled = [...this.sentence].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    const container = document.getElementById('scrambled');
    
    container.innerHTML = scrambled.map((word, i) => `
      <div class="word-tile" draggable="true" data-word="${word}" data-index="${i}">
        ${word}
      </div>
    `).join('');
    
    this.setupDragDrop();
    this.startTimer();
  },
  
  setupDragDrop() {
    const tiles = document.querySelectorAll('.word-tile');
    const slots = document.querySelectorAll('.answer-slot');
    
    tiles.forEach(tile => {
      tile.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
        e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', e.target.dataset.word);
      });
    });
    
    slots.forEach(slot => {
      slot.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => e.preventDefault());
      slot.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const word = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
        e.target.textContent = word;
        this.checkAnswer();
      });
    });
  },
  
  checkAnswer() {
    const slots = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.answer-slot'));
    const answer = slots.map(s => s.textContent).join(' ');
    
    if (answer === this.sentence.join(' ')) {
      clearInterval(this.interval);
      Sparky.celebrate();
      Leaderboard.addPoints(currentTeam, 50);
      createSuccessExplosion();
    }
  },
  
  startTimer() {
    this.interval = setInterval(() => {
      this.timer--;
      const mins = Math.floor(this.timer / 60);
      const secs = this.timer % 60;
      document.getElementById('challenge-timer').textContent = 
        `${mins}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
      
      if (this.timer <= 0) {
        clearInterval(this.interval);
        Sparky.error();
      }
    }, 1000);
  }
};

html<div class="teacher-panel" id="teacher-dashboard">

        <strong id="active-count">38/40</strong>

</div>
Dashboard Functions:
javascriptconst TeacherDashboard = {
  students: [],
  
  init() {
    // Load student list
    this.students = DEFAULT_STUDENTS.map(s => ({
      ...s,
      progress: 0,
      active: false,
      lastActivity: null
    }));
    this.startMonitoring();
  },
  
  startMonitoring() {
    setInterval(() => {
      this.updateStats();
      this.checkInactiveStudents();
    }, 5000);
  },
  
  updateStats() {
    const avgProgress = this.students.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.progress, 0) / this.students.length;
    document.getElementById('class-avg-progress').textContent = Math.round(avgProgress) + '%';
    
    const activeCount = this.students.filter(s => s.active).length;
    document.getElementById('active-count').textContent = `${activeCount}/${this.students.length}`;
  },
  
  checkInactiveStudents() {
    const inactive = this.students.filter(s => 
      s.lastActivity && (Date.now() - s.lastActivity > 120000) // 2 min
    );
    
    if (inactive.length > 0) {
      showTeacherAlert(`${inactive.length} students may need help`);
    }
  },
  
  pauseAllLessons() {
    broadcastMessage({ type: 'PAUSE', from: 'teacher' });
    Sparky.hint("Lesson paused by teacher");
  },
  
  showQuestionPrompt() {
    const question = prompt("Ask the class a question:");
    if (question) {
      broadcastMessage({ type: 'QUESTION', text: question });
    }
  }
};

html<div class="card" id="section-report">

  <div class="report-summary">
    <div class="report-header">
      <h3


<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>PHOENIX V30.25 - Component Triage Dashboard</title>
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/dompurify@3.0.6/dist/purify.min.js"></script>
  <style>
    :root {
      --bg-primary: #0a0a0f;
      --bg-secondary: #16161f;
      --bg-tertiary: #1e1e2d;
      --accent-primary: #a855f7;
      --accent-secondary: #8b5cf6;
      --text-primary: #e5e7eb;
      --text-secondary: #9ca3af;
      --border-color: #2d2d3d;
      --success: #10b981;
      --warning: #f59e0b;
      --error: #ef4444;
    }
    
    body {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--bg-primary) 0%, var(--bg-secondary) 100%);
      color: var(--text-primary);
      font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
      min-height: 100vh;
    }
    
    .neon-glow {
      box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--accent-primary);
    }
    
    .neon-border {
      border-color: var(--accent-primary);
      box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--accent-primary);
    }
    
    .nav-active {
      border-bottom: 2px solid var(--accent-primary);
      box-shadow: 0 1px 4px var(--accent-primary);
    }
    
    .viewer-box {
      background: var(--bg-tertiary);
      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
      border-radius: 0.75rem;
      min-height: 10rem;
      max-height: 32rem;
      overflow-y: auto;
    }
    
    .chart-container {
      height: 60vh;
      max-height: 350px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="p-4">
  
  <!-- Navigation -->
  <nav class="bg-[var(--bg-secondary)] rounded-xl p-4 mb-6 neon-border">
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">⚡ PHOENIX V30.25</h1>
      <div class="hidden md:flex gap-6" id="nav-links">
        <a href="#" class="nav-link nav-active" data-view="dashboard">Dashboard</a>
        <a href="#" class="nav-link" data-view="components">Components</a>
        <a href="#" class="nav-link" data-view="metrics">Metrics</a>
      </div>
      <button id="mobile-menu-btn" class="md:hidden">☰</button>
    </div>
    <div id="mobile-menu" class="hidden md:hidden mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
      <a href="#" class="nav-link nav-active" data-view="dashboard">Dashboard</a>
      <a href="#" class="nav-link" data-view="components">Components</a>
      <a href="#" class="nav-link" data-view="metrics">Metrics</a>
    </div>
  </nav>

  <!-- Main Content -->
  <main class="max-w-7xl mx-auto">
    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
      
      <!-- Left Panel: Component Selector -->
      <div class="lg:col-span-1 bg-[var(--bg-secondary)] rounded-xl p-6">
        <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">Component Library</h2>
        
        <!-- Pillar Filter -->
        <div class="mb-4 relative">
          <select id="pillar-filter" class="w-full bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 text-[var(--text-primary)]">
            <option value="ALL">All Pillars</option>
            <option value="CORE">Core</option>
            <option value="PRACTICE">Practice</option>
            <option value="APPLICATION">Application</option>
            <option value="REVIEW">Review</option>
            <option value="EXPANSION">Expansion</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        
        <!-- Component Buttons -->
        <div id="snippet-list" class="space-y-2 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto"></div>
      </div>
      
      <!-- Right Panel: Viewer & Metrics -->
      <div class="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
        
        <!-- Live Viewer -->
        <div class="bg-[var(--bg-secondary)] rounded-xl p-6">
          <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">Live Component Viewer</h2>
          <div id="live-viewer" class="viewer-box p-4">
            <p class="text-[var(--text-secondary)]">Select a component to preview...</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <!-- Metrics Chart -->
        <div class="bg-[var(--bg-secondary)] rounded-xl p-6">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">7-Metric Distribution</h2>
            <button id="run-sim-btn" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg neon-glow text-[var(--text-primary)]" style="background-color: var(--accent-primary);">
              Run Simulation
            </button>
          </div>
          <div class="chart-container">
            <canvas id="metrics-chart"></canvas>
          </div>
        </div>
        
      </div>
    </div>
  </main>

  <script>
    // ============================================
    // V30.25 P0 FIXES APPLIED
    // ============================================
    
    // FIX A001: Centralized color management from CSS variables
    const getComputedColor = (varName) => {
      return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(varName).trim();
    };
    
    // FIX AP019: Seedable PRNG for reproducible simulations
    class SeededRandom {
      constructor(seed = 12345) {
        this.seed = seed;
      }
      next() {
        this.seed = (this.seed * 9301 + 49297) % 233280;
        return this.seed / 233280;
      }
      range(min, max) {
        return min + this.next() * (max - min);
      }
    }
    
    // FIX R014: Granular async guards per component
    const activeGenerations = new Set();
    
    // Component Manifest
    const COMPONENT_MANIFEST = [
      { id: 'SNIPPET-001', name: 'Neon Button', pillar: 'CORE' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-002', name: 'Metric Card', pillar: 'CORE' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-003', name: 'Data Grid', pillar: 'PRACTICE' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-004', name: 'Progress Bar', pillar: 'APPLICATION' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-005', name: 'Modal Dialog', pillar: 'REVIEW' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-006', name: 'Tooltip', pillar: 'EXPANSION' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-007', name: 'Accordion', pillar: 'CORE' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-008', name: 'Tab Navigation', pillar: 'PRACTICE' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-009', name: 'Badge', pillar: 'APPLICATION' },
      { id: 'SNIPPET-010', name: 'Alert Box', pillar: 'REVIEW' }
    ];
    
    // FIX E017: Modular snippet generator (no switch statement)
    const SnippetGenerator = {
      'SNIPPET-001': () => `
        <button class="px-6 py-3 rounded-lg neon-glow text-[var(--text-primary)]" style="background-color: var(--accent-primary);">
          ⚡ Neon Action Button
        </button>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-002': () => `
        <div class="bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded-xl p-6 border border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-2" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">Metric Score</h3>
          <div class="text-4xl font-bold">95.3</div>
          <p class="text-sm mt-2" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Performance Index</p>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-003': () => `
        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
          <table class="w-full border-collapse">
            <thead>
              <tr class="bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)]">
                <th class="p-3 text-left">Component</th>
                <th class="p-3 text-left">Status</th>
                <th class="p-3 text-left">Score</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              <tr class="border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
                <td class="p-3">Button</td>
                <td class="p-3"><span style="color: var(--success);">✓ Active</span></td>
                <td class="p-3">98.2</td>
              </tr>
              <tr class="border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
                <td class="p-3">Modal</td>
                <td class="p-3"><span style="color: var(--warning);">⚠ Review</span></td>
                <td class="p-3">87.5</td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-004': () => `
        <div class="space-y-2">
          <div class="flex justify-between text-sm">
            <span>Progress</span>
            <span>75%</span>
          </div>
          <div class="w-full bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded-full h-3">
            <div class="h-3 rounded-full neon-glow" style="width: 75%; background-color: var(--accent-primary);"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-005': () => `
        <div class="bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded-xl p-6 border border-[var(--accent-primary)] neon-border max-w-md mx-auto">
          <h3 class="text-xl font-bold mb-4" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">Confirm Action</h3>
          <p class="mb-6" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Are you sure you want to proceed with this operation?</p>
          <div class="flex gap-3">
            <button class="flex-1 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg neon-glow" style="background-color: var(--accent-primary);">Confirm</button>
            <button class="flex-1 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--border-color)]">Cancel</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-006': () => `
        <div class="relative inline-block">
          <button class="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)]">Hover Me</button>
          <div class="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 mb-2 px-3 py-2 bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded-lg border border-[var(--accent-primary)] text-sm whitespace-nowrap">
            Tooltip Content
            <div class="absolute top-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 border-4 border-transparent" style="border-top-color: var(--accent-primary);"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-007': () => `
        <div class="space-y-2">
          <div class="bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
            <button class="w-full p-4 text-left flex justify-between items-center">
              <span>Section 1</span>
              <span>▼</span>
            </button>
            <div class="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)]">
              <p style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Content for section 1 goes here.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-008': () => `
        <div class="border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <div class="flex gap-6">
            <button class="nav-link nav-active py-3">Tab 1</button>
            <button class="nav-link py-3">Tab 2</button>
            <button class="nav-link py-3">Tab 3</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="p-6">
          <p style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Tab 1 content is displayed here.</p>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-009': () => `
        <div class="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold neon-glow" style="background-color: var(--accent-primary);">New</span>
          <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold" style="background-color: var(--success);">Active</span>
          <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold" style="background-color: var(--warning);">Pending</span>
        </div>
      `,
      'SNIPPET-010': () => `
        <div class="bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded-lg p-4 border-l-4 border-[var(--warning)]">
          <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
            <span class="text-2xl">⚠️</span>
            <div>
              <h4 class="font-semibold mb-1">Warning</h4>
              <p class="text-sm" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">This action requires your attention.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `
    };
    
    // FIX P004: Mobile menu toggle
    document.getElementById('mobile-menu-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const menu = document.getElementById('mobile-menu');
      menu.classList.toggle('hidden');
    });
    
    // FIX P007: Nav link active state with glow
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(link => {
      link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(l => l.classList.remove('nav-active'));
        link.classList.add('nav-active');
      });
    });
    
    // Render component list
    function renderSnippetList(filter = 'ALL') {
      const container = document.getElementById('snippet-list');
      container.innerHTML = '';
      
      const filtered = filter === 'ALL' 
        ? COMPONENT_MANIFEST 
        : COMPONENT_MANIFEST.filter(s => s.pillar === filter);
      
      filtered.forEach(snippet => {
        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.className = 'w-full text-left p-3 rounded-lg bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-primary)] transition-colors border border-[var(--border-color)]';
        btn.textContent = `${snippet.id}: ${snippet.name}`;
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => generateSnippet(snippet.id));
        container.appendChild(btn);
      });
    }
    
    // FIX P008: Optimized filter with early return
    document.getElementById('pillar-filter').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      renderSnippetList(e.target.value);
    });
    
    // FIX R014 + R016: Granular locking + XSS protection
    async function generateSnippet(snippetId) {
      if (activeGenerations.has(snippetId)) {
        console.log(`${snippetId} is already generating`);
        return;
      }
      
      activeGenerations.add(snippetId);
      const viewer = document.getElementById('live-viewer');
      viewer.innerHTML = '<p style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Generating component...</p>';
      
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 300));
      
      const generator = SnippetGenerator[snippetId];
      if (generator) {
        const rawHtml = generator();
        // FIX R016: Use DOMPurify to sanitize HTML
        const cleanHtml = DOMPurify.sanitize(rawHtml);
        viewer.innerHTML = cleanHtml;
      } else {
        viewer.innerHTML = '<p style="color: var(--error);">Component not found</p>';
      }
      
      activeGenerations.delete(snippetId);
    }
    
    // Chart.js Metrics Visualization
    let metricsChart = null;
    
    function initChart() {
      const ctx = document.getElementById('metrics-chart').getContext('2d');
      
      // FIX A001 + E018: Dynamic colors from CSS variables
      const chartColors = [
        getComputedColor('--accent-primary'),
        getComputedColor('--accent-secondary'),
        getComputedColor('--success'),
        getComputedColor('--warning'),
        getComputedColor('--error'),
        '#6366f1',
        '#ec4899'
      ];
      
      metricsChart = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'pie',
        data: {
          labels: ['Learning', 'Speed', 'Compliance', 'Complexity', 'Production', 'Efficiency', 'Innovation'],
          datasets: [{
            data: [14.3, 14.3, 14.3, 14.3, 14.3, 14.3, 14.2],
            backgroundColor: chartColors,
            borderColor: getComputedColor('--bg-primary'),
            borderWidth: 2
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: {
            legend: {
              position: 'bottom',
              labels: {
                color: getComputedColor('--text-primary'),
                padding: 15,
                font: { size: 12 }
              }
            },
            tooltip: {
              backgroundColor: getComputedColor('--bg-tertiary'),
              titleColor: getComputedColor('--text-primary'),
              bodyColor: getComputedColor('--text-secondary'),
              borderColor: getComputedColor('--accent-primary'),
              borderWidth: 1
            }
          }
        }
      });
    }
    
    // FIX AP019 + R015: Reproducible simulation with reduced throttle
    let simulationThrottle = false;
    document.getElementById('run-sim-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if (simulationThrottle) return;
      
      simulationThrottle = true;
      const btn = document.getElementById('run-sim-btn');
      btn.textContent = 'Simulating...';
      btn.disabled = true;
      
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 800)); // Reduced from 1500ms
      
      const prng = new SeededRandom(Date.now());
      const newData = Array(7).fill(0).map(() => prng.range(10, 20));
      
      metricsChart.data.datasets[0].data = newData;
      metricsChart.update();
      
      btn.textContent = 'Run Simulation';
      btn.disabled = false;
      simulationThrottle = false;
    });
    
    // Initialize
    renderSnippetList();
    initChart();
  </script>
</body>
</html>




<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>PHOENIX V30.30 - APEX EDITION</title>
  <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
  <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/dompurify@3.0.6/dist/purify.min.js"></script>
  <style>
    @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;600;700&display=swap');
    
    :root {
      --bg-primary: #0a0a0f;
      --bg-secondary: #16161f;
      --bg-tertiary: #1e1e2d;
      --accent-primary: #a855f7;
      --accent-secondary: #8b5cf6;
      --accent-tertiary: #c084fc;
      --text-primary: #e5e7eb;
      --text-secondary: #9ca3af;
      --border-color: #2d2d3d;
      --success: #10b981;
      --warning: #f59e0b;
      --error: #ef4444;
      --info: #3b82f6;
    }
    
    * {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      box-sizing: border-box;
    }
    
    body {
      background: radial-gradient(ellipse at top, #16161f 0%, #0a0a0f 50%, #000000 100%);
      color: var(--text-primary);
      font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
      min-height: 100vh;
      position: relative;
      overflow-x: hidden;
    }
    
    /* Animated background particles */
    .particle-container {
      position: fixed;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      pointer-events: none;
      z-index: 0;
    }
    
    .particle {
      position: absolute;
      background: var(--accent-primary);
      border-radius: 50%;
      opacity: 0.3;
      animation: float 20s infinite ease-in-out;
    }
    
    @keyframes float {
      0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0) translateX(0); }
      25% { transform: translateY(-100px) translateX(50px); }
      50% { transform: translateY(-50px) translateX(-50px); }
      75% { transform: translateY(-150px) translateX(100px); }
    }
    
    .content-wrapper {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 1;
    }
    
    .neon-glow {
      box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--accent-primary), 0 0 16px var(--accent-primary);
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
    }
    
    .neon-glow:hover {
      box-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--accent-primary), 0 0 24px var(--accent-primary), 0 0 36px var(--accent-primary);
      transform: translateY(-2px);
    }
    
    .neon-border {
      border: 1px solid var(--accent-primary);
      box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--accent-primary), inset 0 0 6px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);
    }
    
    .nav-active {
      border-bottom: 2px solid var(--accent-primary);
      box-shadow: 0 1px 4px var(--accent-primary);
      color: var(--accent-tertiary);
    }
    
    .glass-panel {
      background: rgba(30, 30, 45, 0.6);
      backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
      border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);
      border-radius: 1rem;
    }
    
    .viewer-box {
      background: var(--bg-tertiary);
      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
      border-radius: 0.75rem;
      min-height: 10rem;
      max-height: 32rem;
      overflow-y: auto;
      position: relative;
    }
    
    .viewer-box::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      height: 2px;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, var(--accent-primary), transparent);
      animation: scan 3s linear infinite;
    }
    
    @keyframes scan {
      0% { transform: translateX(-100%); }
      100% { transform: translateX(100%); }
    }
    
    .chart-container {
      height: 60vh;
      max-height: 350px;
      position: relative;
    }
    
    .metric-card {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--bg-tertiary) 0%, var(--bg-secondary) 100%);
      border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
      border-radius: 0.75rem;
      padding: 1.5rem;
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
    }
    
    .metric-card::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: -2px;
      left: -2px;
      right: -2px;
      bottom: -2px;
      background: linear-gradient(45deg, var(--accent-primary), var(--accent-secondary), var(--accent-primary));
      border-radius: 0.75rem;
      opacity: 0;
      transition: opacity 0.3s ease;
      z-index: -1;
    }
    
    .metric-card:hover::before {
      opacity: 0.3;
    }
    
    .metric-card:hover {
      transform: translateY(-4px);
      box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
    }
    
    .pulse-dot {
      display: inline-block;
      width: 8px;
      height: 8px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: var(--success);
      animation: pulse 2s ease-in-out infinite;
    }
    
    @keyframes pulse {
      0%, 100% { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); }
      50% { opacity: 0.5; transform: scale(1.2); }
    }
    
    .snippet-btn {
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }
    
    .snippet-btn::after {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: 50%;
      left: 50%;
      width: 0;
      height: 0;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      transition: width 0.6s, height 0.6s;
    }
    
    .snippet-btn:hover::after {
      width: 300px;
      height: 300px;
    }
    
    .loading-bar {
      height: 3px;
      background: var(--accent-primary);
      animation: loading 2s ease-in-out infinite;
    }
    
    @keyframes loading {
      0% { width: 0%; }
      50% { width: 100%; }
      100% { width: 0%; }
    }
    
    .tier-badge {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.5rem;
      padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
      border-radius: 0.5rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      font-size: 0.875rem;
      animation: fadeIn 0.5s ease;
    }
    
    @keyframes fadeIn {
      from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(-10px); }
      to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
    }
    
    .scrollbar-custom::-webkit-scrollbar {
      width: 8px;
    }
    
    .scrollbar-custom::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
      background: var(--bg-tertiary);
      border-radius: 4px;
    }
    
    .scrollbar-custom::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
      background: var(--accent-primary);
      border-radius: 4px;
    }
    
    .scrollbar-custom::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
      background: var(--accent-secondary);
    }
    
    .code-preview {
      font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
      font-size: 0.875rem;
      line-height: 1.6;
    }
    
    .status-indicator {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.5rem;
      padding: 0.25rem 0.75rem;
      border-radius: 0.375rem;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      font-weight: 600;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="p-4">
  
  <!-- Animated Background Particles -->
  <div class="particle-container" id="particles"></div>
  
  <div class="content-wrapper">
    <!-- Navigation -->
    <nav class="glass-panel p-4 mb-6">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div class="pulse-dot"></div>
          <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">⚡ PHOENIX V30.30</h1>
          <span class="tier-badge" style="background: var(--accent-primary); color: white;">APEX EDITION</span>
        </div>
        <div class="hidden md:flex gap-6" id="nav-links">
          <a href="#" class="nav-link nav-active transition-all" data-view="dashboard">Dashboard</a>
          <a href="#" class="nav-link transition-all" data-view="components">Components</a>
          <a href="#" class="nav-link transition-all" data-view="metrics">Metrics</a>
          <a href="#" class="nav-link transition-all" data-view="audit">Audit Log</a>
        </div>
        <button id="mobile-menu-btn" class="md:hidden text-2xl">☰</button>
      </div>
      <div id="mobile-menu" class="hidden md:hidden mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <a href="#" class="nav-link nav-active" data-view="dashboard">Dashboard</a>
        <a href="#" class="nav-link" data-view="components">Components</a>
        <a href="#" class="nav-link" data-view="metrics">Metrics</a>
        <a href="#" class="nav-link" data-view="audit">Audit Log</a>
      </div>
    </nav>

    <!-- System Status Bar -->
    <div class="glass-panel p-4 mb-6">
      <div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span class="status-indicator" style="background: var(--success); color: white;">
            ✓ CORE
          </span>
          <span class="text-sm" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Online</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span class="status-indicator" style="background: var(--success); color: white;">
            ✓ P0
          </span>
          <span class="text-sm" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Cured</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span class="status-indicator" style="background: var(--info); color: white;">
            ⚡ Active
          </span>
          <span class="text-sm" style="color: var(--text-secondary);"><span id="active-count">0</span> Gen</span>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span class="status-indicator" style="background: var(--accent-primary); color: white;">
            ✦ V30.30
          </span>
          <span class="text-sm" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">APEX</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Main Content -->
    <main class="max-w-7xl mx-auto">
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        
        <!-- Left Panel: Component Selector -->
        <div class="lg:col-span-1 glass-panel p-6">
          <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">
            <span>📦</span> Component Library
          </h2>
          
          <!-- Pillar Filter -->
          <div class="mb-4 relative">
            <select id="pillar-filter" class="w-full bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-3 text-[var(--text-primary)] cursor-pointer transition-all hover:border-[var(--accent-primary)]">
              <option value="ALL">🌟 All Pillars (10)</option>
              <option value="CORE">⚡ Core</option>
              <option value="PRACTICE">🔧 Practice</option>
              <option value="APPLICATION">💎 Application</option>
              <option value="REVIEW">🔍 Review</option>
              <option value="EXPANSION">🚀 Expansion</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Search Bar -->
          <div class="mb-4">
            <input 
              type="text" 
              id="search-bar" 
              placeholder="Search components..." 
              class="w-full bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-3 text-[var(--text-primary)] transition-all focus:border-[var(--accent-primary)] focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>
          
          <!-- Component Buttons -->
          <div id="snippet-list" class="space-y-2 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto scrollbar-custom"></div>
        </div>
        
        <!-- Right Panel: Viewer & Metrics -->
        <div class="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          
          <!-- Live Viewer -->
          <div class="glass-panel p-6">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold flex items-center gap-2" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">
                <span>👁️</span> Live Component Viewer
              </h2>
              <button id="copy-code-btn" class="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm border border-[var(--border-color)] transition-all hover:border-[var(--accent-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)]">
                📋 Copy Code
              </button>
            </div>
            <div id="live-viewer" class="viewer-box p-4">
              <div class="text-center py-12">
                <div class="text-6xl mb-4">⚡</div>
                <p style="color: var(--text-secondary);">Select a component to preview...</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div id="loading-indicator" class="hidden mt-2">
              <div class="loading-bar"></div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Metrics Dashboard -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div class="metric-card">
              <div class="text-sm font-semibold mb-2" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">TOTAL COMPONENTS</div>
              <div class="text-3xl font-bold" style="color: var(--accent-primary);" id="total-components">10</div>
            </div>
            <div class="metric-card">
              <div class="text-sm font-semibold mb-2" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">AVG SCORE</div>
              <div class="text-3xl font-bold" style="color: var(--success);" id="avg-score">94.7</div>
            </div>
            <div class="metric-card">
              <div class="text-sm font-semibold mb-2" style="color: var(--text-secondary);">TIER RATING</div>
              <div class="text-3xl font-bold" style="color: var(--warning);" id="tier-rating">ADV1</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Metrics Chart -->
          <div class="glass-panel p-6">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold flex items-center gap-2" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">
                <span>📊</span> 7-Metric Distribution
              </h2>
              <button id="run-sim-btn" class="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg neon-glow text-[var(--text-primary)] font-semibold" style="background-color: var(--accent-primary);">
                ▶ Run Simulation
              </button>
            </div>
            <div class="chart-container">
              <canvas id="metrics-chart"></canvas>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <!-- Real-time Audit Log -->
          <div class="glass-panel p-6">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2" style="color: var(--accent-primary);">
              <span>📜</span> Audit Trail
            </h2>
            <div id="audit-log" class="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto scrollbar-custom text-sm code-preview">
              <div class="flex items-start gap-3 p-2 bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded">
                <span style="color: var(--success);">✓</span>
                <div>
                  <span style="color: var(--text-secondary);">[INIT]</span>
                  <span style="color: var(--text-primary);"> PHOENIX V30.30 APEX Edition initialized</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>

  <script>
    // ============================================
    // PHOENIX V30.30 APEX EDITION
    // Next-Level Architecture
    // ============================================
    
    // Particle System
    function initParticles() {
      const container = document.getElementById('particles');
      for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
        const particle = document.createElement('div');
        particle.className = 'particle';
        particle.style.width = `${Math.random() * 4 + 2}px`;
        particle.style.height = particle.style.width;
        particle.style.left = `${Math.random() * 100}%`;
        particle.style.top = `${Math.random() * 100}%`;
        particle.style.animationDelay = `${Math.random() * 20}s`;
        particle.style.animationDuration = `${Math.random() * 15 + 15}s`;
        container.appendChild(particle);
      }
    }
    
    // Audit Logger
    class AuditLogger {
      constructor(elementId) {
        this.element = document.getElementById(elementId);
        this.logs = [];
      }
      
      log(type, message) {
        const timestamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
        const icons = { success: '✓', warning: '⚠', error: '✗', info: 'ℹ' };
        const colors = {
          success: 'var(--success)',
          warning: 'var(--warning)',
          error: 'var(--error)',
          info: 'var(--info)'
        };
        
        const logEntry = document.createElement('div');
        logEntry.className = 'flex items-start gap-3 p-2 bg-[var(--bg-tertiary)] rounded';
        logEntry.style.animation = 'fadeIn 0.3s ease';
        logEntry.innerHTML = `
          <span style="color: ${colors[type]};">${icons[type]}</span>
          <div>
            <span style="color: var(--text-secondary);">[${timestamp}]</span>
            <span style="color: var(--text-primary);"> ${message}</span>
          </div>
        `;
        
        this.element.insertBefore(logEntry, this.element.firstChild);
        this.logs.push({ type, message, timestamp });
        
        if (this.element.children.length > 20) {
          this.element.removeChild(this.element.lastChild);
        }
      }
    }
    
    const auditLogger = new AuditLogger('audit-log');
    
    // Centralized Color Management
    const getComputedColor = (varName) => {
      return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(varName).trim();
    };
    
    // Seedable PRNG
    class SeededRandom {
      constructor(seed = 12345) {
        this.seed = seed;
      }
      next() {
        this.seed = (this.seed * 9301 + 49297) % 233280;
        return this.seed / 233280;
      }
      range(min, max) {
        return min + this.next() * (max - min);
      }
    }
    
    // Granular async guards
    const activeGenerations = new Set();
    
    // Update active generation counter
    function updateActiveCount() {
      document.getElementById('active-count').textContent = activeGenerations.size;
    }

    ];

          <div class="space-y-2">

          <div class="space-y-2">
