    <div class="card hidden" id="section-feedback">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>⭐</span> Lesson Feedback</h2>
      
      <p style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">How was today's lesson? Your feedback helps us improve!</p>
      
      <div class="feedback-stars" id="stars">
        <span onclick="rate(1)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(2)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(3)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(4)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(5)">⭐</span>
      </div>
      
      <textarea id="comment" placeholder="Any comments or suggestions? What did you enjoy most?"></textarea>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="exportFeedback()" style="margin-top: 2rem;">📥 Export Feedback JSON</button>
      
      <div class="score-display" style="margin-top: 3rem;">
        <div class="score-item">
          <div class="score-value">90 min</div>
          <div class="score-label">Completed</div>
        </div>
        <div class="score-item">
          <div class="score-value" id="final-score">0%</div>
          <div class="score-label">Overall Score</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

  </div>

  <script>
    const students = ['Nguyễn Lê Phượng Nhi','Trương Thị Quỳnh Hương','Nhật Lam','Huỳnh Gia Hãn','Khánh Duy','Gia An','Thái Vi','Trần Anh Thư','Võ Ngọc Mỹ Hoàng','Đặng Quỳnh Giang'];
    const feedback = {vocab:[],quiz:[],games:{wheel:[],reflex:0,memory:0},rating:0,comment:''};
    let quizScore = 0;
    let reflexScore = 0;
    let memoryScore = 0;
    let currentSection = 0;
    const totalSections = 7;

    function updateProgress() {
      currentSection++;
      const pct = (currentSection / totalSections) * 100;
      document.getElementById('overall-progress').style.width = pct + '%';
      document.getElementById('progress-text').textContent = Math.round(pct) + '%';
    }

    function nextSection(id) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(c => c.classList.add('hidden'));
      document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('hidden');
      updateProgress();
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }

    function playAudio(word) {
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(word);
      utterance.lang = 'en-US';
      speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
      feedback.vocab.push(word);
    }

    function answerQuiz(el, correct, qNum) {
      const siblings = el.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.quiz-option');
      siblings.forEach(s => s.style.pointerEvents = 'none');
      el.classList.add(correct ? 'quiz-correct


<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>NET Lesson: The Shepherd's Trick</title>
  <style>
    :root {
      --neon-purple: #a855f7;
      --deep-purple: #7e22ce;
      --glow-purple: #c084fc;
      --gold: #f59e0b;
      --deep-gold: #d97706;
      --dark: #0f0f1a;
      --darker: #0a0a0f;
      --border: #27273a;
      --text: #e5e7eb;
      --text-dim: #9ca3af;
    }
    * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
    body {
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--darker), #0d0a14, var(--darker));
      color: var(--text);
      font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
      min-height: 100vh;
      position: relative;
      overflow-x: hidden;
    }
    .blob {
      position: fixed;
      width: 600px;
      height: 600px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(168,85,247,0.3), transparent);
      border-radius: 40% 60% 70% 30% / 40% 50% 60% 50%;
      filter: blur(80px);
      pointer-events: none;
      animation: morph 15s ease-in-out infinite;
      z-index: 0;
    }
    .blob1 { top: -200px; left: -200px; }
    .blob2 { bottom: -200px; right: -200px; background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(245,158,11,0.3), transparent); }
    @keyframes morph {
      0%, 100% { border-radius: 40% 60% 70% 30% / 40% 50% 60% 50%; }
      50% { border-radius: 60% 40% 30% 70% / 50% 60% 40% 50%; }
    }
    .container {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 1;
      max-width: 1200px;
      margin: 0 auto;
      padding: 2rem;
    }
    .hero {
      text-align: center;
      margin-bottom: 3rem;
      padding: 3rem 2rem;
      background: rgba(26,26,38,0.6);
      backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
      border: 2px solid var(--neon-purple);
      border-radius: 1rem;
      box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(168,85,247,0.3);
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }
    .hero::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      top: 0;
      left: 0;
      right: 0;
      height: 3px;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--gold), var(--neon-purple));
      box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--neon-purple);
    }
    .hero-title {
      font-size: clamp(2rem, 6vw, 4rem);
      font-weight: 800;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--glow-purple), var(--gold));
      -webkit-background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }
    .hero-subtitle {
      font-size: 1.25rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
    }
    .card {
      background: rgba(26,26,38,0.7);
      backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
      border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,0.2);
      border-radius: 1rem;
      padding: 2rem;
      margin-bottom: 2rem;
      box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
      transition: all 0.3s;
    }
    .card:hover {
      transform: translateY(-4px);
      box-shadow: 0 12px 48px rgba(168,85,247,0.4);
    }
    .card-title {
      font-size: 1.5rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      color: var(--glow-purple);
      margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.75rem;
    }
    .btn {
      padding: 1rem 2rem;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--deep-purple));
      color: white;
      border: 0;
      border-radius: 0.75rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      cursor: pointer;
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px var(--neon-purple);
      transition: all 0.3s;
      font-size: 1rem;
    }
    .btn:hover {
      transform: translateY(-2px);
      box-shadow: 0 0 40px var(--neon-purple);
    }
    .btn-ghost {
      background: transparent;
      border: 2px solid var(--neon-purple);
      color: var(--glow-purple);
    }
    .vocab-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
      gap: 1.5rem;
      margin: 2rem 0;
    }
    .vocab-card {
      background: rgba(15,15,26,0.8);
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 1rem;
      padding: 1.5rem;
      text-align: center;
      transition: all 0.3s;
    }
    .vocab-card:hover {
      border-color: var(--neon-purple);
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168,85,247,0.3);
      transform: translateY(-4px);
    }
    .vocab-word {
      font-size: 1.5rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      color: var(--gold);
      text-shadow: 0 0 15px var(--gold);
      margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
    }
    .vocab-def {
      color: var(--text-dim);
      font-size: 0.875rem;
      line-height: 1.6;
    }
    .reading {
      background: rgba(15,15,26,0.6);
      border-left: 4px solid var(--gold);
      padding: 1.5rem;
      border-radius: 0.5rem;
      margin: 2rem 0;
      line-height: 1.8;
      font-size: 1.1rem;
    }
    .reading p {
      margin-bottom: 1rem;
    }
    .reading strong {
      color: var(--gold);
      text-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--gold);
    }
    .quiz-option {
      padding: 1rem;
      background: rgba(34,34,58,0.6);
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 0.75rem;
      margin: 0.5rem 0;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: all 0.3s;
    }
    .quiz-option:hover {
      background: rgba(168,85,247,0.1);
      border-color: var(--neon-purple);
    }
    .quiz-correct {
      background: rgba(34,197,94,0.2) !important;
      border-color: #22c55e !important;
    }
    .quiz-wrong {
      background: rgba(239,68,68,0.2) !important;
      border-color: #ef4444 !important;
    }
    .game-area {
      background: rgba(15,15,26,0.8);
      border: 2px solid var(--neon-purple);
      border-radius: 1rem;
      padding: 2rem;
      text-align: center;
      box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(168,85,247,0.3);
      min-height: 300px;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
    }
    .wheel {
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 400px;
      height: 300px;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(168,85,247,0.1), rgba(245,158,11,0.1));
      border: 2px solid var(--neon-purple);
      border-radius: 1rem;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 2rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      color: var(--gold);
      text-shadow: 0 0 20px var(--gold);
      margin: 2rem auto;
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }
    .wheel::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      inset: 0;
      background: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent, rgba(168,85,247,0.3), transparent);
      animation: shimmer 2s linear infinite;
    }
    @keyframes shimmer {
      0% { transform: translateX(-100%); }
      100% { transform: translateX(100%); }
    }
    .progress {
      width: 100%;
      height: 12px;
      background: var(--dark);
      border-radius: 6px;
      overflow: hidden;
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      margin: 1rem 0;
    }
    .progress-fill {
      height: 100%;
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--gold));
      border-radius: 6px;
      box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--neon-purple);
      transition: width 1s ease-out;
    }
    .timer {
      font-size: 3rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      color: var(--gold);
      text-shadow: 0 0 20px var(--gold);
      margin: 2rem 0;
    }
    .score-display {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-around;
      gap: 2rem;
      margin: 2rem 0;
    }
    .score-item {
      text-align: center;
    }
    .score-value {
      font-size: 2.5rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--gold));
      -webkit-background-clip: text;
      -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
    }
    .score-label {
      color: var(--text-dim);
      font-size: 0.875rem;
      margin-top: 0.5rem;
    }
    .hidden {
      display: none;
    }
    .feedback-stars {
      display: flex;
      gap: 1rem;
      justify-content: center;
      margin: 2rem 0;
      font-size: 2rem;
    }
    .feedback-stars span {
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: all 0.3s;
      filter: grayscale(100%);
    }
    .feedback-stars span:hover,
    .feedback-stars span.active {
      filter: grayscale(0%) drop-shadow(0 0 10px var(--gold));
      transform: scale(1.2);
    }
    textarea {
      width: 100%;
      min-height: 120px;
      background: rgba(15,15,26,0.8);
      border: 1px solid var(--border);
      border-radius: 0.75rem;
      padding: 1rem;
      color: var(--text);
      font-size: 1rem;
      resize: vertical;
    }
    textarea:focus {
      outline: none;
      border-color: var(--neon-purple);
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168,85,247,0.3);
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  
  <div class="blob blob1"></div>
  <div class="blob blob2"></div>

  <div class="container">
    
    <div class="hero">
      <div style="font-size: 4rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">⚡</div>
      <h1 class="hero-title">The Shepherd's Trick</h1>
      <p class="hero-subtitle">A 90-Minute Natural English Training Lesson</p>
      <div class="progress">
        <div class="progress-fill" id="overall-progress" style="width: 0%"></div>
      </div>
      <p style="color: var(--text-dim); font-size: 0.875rem;">Progress: <span id="progress-text">0%</span></p>
    </div>

    <!-- SECTION 1: INTRODUCTION & VOCABULARY (15 min) -->
    <div class="card" id="section-vocab">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>📚</span> Vocabulary Builder</h2>
      <p style="margin-bottom: 2rem; line-height: 1.6;">Let's learn 8 key words from today's story. Click each card to hear pronunciation.</p>
      
      <div class="vocab-grid">
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('shepherd')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Shepherd</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">Someone who takes care of sheep</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('bored')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Bored</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">Feeling tired and uninterested</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('villagers')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Villagers</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">People who live in a village</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('shouted')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Shouted</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">Spoke very loudly</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('trick')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Trick</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">A clever joke or deception</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('trust')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Trust</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">Believing someone is honest</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('consequence')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Consequence</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">The result of an action</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
        <div class="vocab-card" onclick="playAudio('lesson')">
          <div class="vocab-word">Lesson</div>
          <div class="vocab-def">Something important you learn</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 0.5rem; color: var(--neon-purple);">🔊 Click to hear</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="nextSection('section-reading')" style="margin-top: 2rem;">Continue to Reading →</button>
    </div>

    <!-- SECTION 2: READING (20 min) -->
    <div class="card hidden" id="section-reading">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>📖</span> The Story: The Shepherd's Trick</h2>
      
      <div class="reading">
        <p><strong>Part 1: A Boring Day</strong></p>
        <p>Once upon a time, in a small village surrounded by green hills, there lived a young shepherd named Tom. Every day, Tom took his sheep to the meadow to graze. He sat on a rock and watched them eat grass. Day after day, it was the same routine. Tom grew very <strong>bored</strong>.</p>
        
        <p><strong>Part 2: The First Trick</strong></p>
        <p>One sunny afternoon, Tom had an idea. "I wonder what would happen if I played a little trick," he thought. He stood up and <strong>shouted</strong> as loud as he could: "Wolf! Wolf! A wolf is attacking my sheep!"</p>
        <p>The <strong>villagers</strong> heard his cries and came running with sticks and tools to help. But when they arrived, they saw no wolf—only Tom laughing. "Got you!" he said with a big smile. The villagers were not happy, but they went back to their work.</p>
        
        <p><strong>Part 3: The Second Trick</strong></p>
        <p>The next day, Tom decided to try his <strong>trick</strong> again. "Wolf! Wolf!" he cried. Again, the villagers rushed to help. And again, there was no wolf. Tom thought it was hilarious, but the villagers felt foolish and angry.</p>
        
        <p><strong>Part 4: The Real Wolf</strong></p>
        <p>On the third day, something terrible happened. A real wolf appeared! It crept toward Tom's sheep with hungry eyes. Tom panicked and shouted, "Wolf! Wolf! Please help!" But this time, no one came. The villagers heard him but thought, "He's tricking us again. We won't fall for it this time."</p>
        <p>The wolf chased away all of Tom's sheep. Tom learned a hard <strong>lesson</strong> that day: when you lie, people lose <strong>trust</strong> in you. And when you really need help, no one will believe you.</p>
        
        <p><strong>Part 5: The Consequence</strong></p>
        <p>Tom sat alone on his rock, watching the empty meadow. He realized that his jokes had serious <strong>consequences</strong>. From that day forward, he promised to always tell the truth and never trick people again.</p>
      </div>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="nextSection('section-quiz')">Continue to Quiz →</button>
    </div>

    <!-- SECTION 3: COMPREHENSION QUIZ (10 min) -->
    <div class="card hidden" id="section-quiz">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>🎯</span> Comprehension Quiz</h2>
      
      <div style="margin: 2rem 0;">
        <p style="font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 1rem;">1. Why did Tom shout "Wolf!" the first time?</p>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, true, 1)">He was bored and wanted attention</div>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 1)">A real wolf was attacking</div>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 1)">He wanted to scare the villagers</div>
      </div>

      <div style="margin: 2rem 0;">
        <p style="font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 1rem;">2. What happened when the real wolf came?</p>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 2)">The villagers saved all the sheep</div>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, true, 2)">No one believed Tom and the sheep were lost</div>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 2)">Tom fought the wolf himself</div>
      </div>

      <div style="margin: 2rem 0;">
        <p style="font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 1rem;">3. What is the main lesson of this story?</p>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 3)">Never work as a shepherd</div>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, true, 3)">Lying destroys trust and has consequences</div>
        <div class="quiz-option" onclick="answerQuiz(this, false, 3)">Wolves are dangerous animals</div>
      </div>

      <div class="score-display">
        <div class="score-item">
          <div class="score-value" id="quiz-score">0/3</div>
          <div class="score-label">Correct Answers</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="nextSection('section-game1')">Continue to Game 1 →</button>
    </div>

    <!-- GAME 1: WHEEL OF NAMES (10 min) -->
    <div class="card hidden" id="section-game1">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>🎡</span> Game 1: Wheel of Names</h2>
      <p style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">Let's pick students randomly to practice speaking! Click SPIN to start.</p>
      
      <div class="game-area">
        <div class="wheel" id="wheel">Press SPIN</div>
        <div style="margin-top: 2rem; display: flex; gap: 1rem;">
          <button class="btn" onclick="spinWheel()">🔥 SPIN</button>
          <button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="stopWheel()">STOP</button>
        </div>
        <div id="picked-student" style="margin-top: 2rem; font-size: 1.5rem; color: var(--gold); font-weight: 800;"></div>
      </div>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="nextSection('section-game2')" style="margin-top: 2rem;">Continue to Game 2 →</button>
    </div>

    <!-- GAME 2: REFLEX TEST (15 min) -->
    <div class="card hidden" id="section-game2">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>⚡</span> Game 2: English Reflex Test</h2>
      <p style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">When you see a GREEN word, click it as fast as you can! Test your English vocabulary speed.</p>
      
      <div class="game-area">
        <div id="reflex-word" style="font-size: 3rem; font-weight: 800; color: var(--text-dim); min-height: 100px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;">
          Ready?
        </div>
        <button class="btn" onclick="startReflexGame()" style="margin-top: 2rem;">Start Reflex Test</button>
        <div class="timer" id="reflex-time">0 ms</div>
        <div class="score-display">
          <div class="score-item">
            <div class="score-value" id="reflex-score">0/5</div>
            <div class="score-label">Correct Clicks</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="nextSection('section-game3')" style="margin-top: 2rem;">Continue to Game 3 →</button>
    </div>

    <!-- GAME 3: WORD MEMORY (15 min) -->
    <div class="card hidden" id="section-game3">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>🧠</span> Game 3: Memory Match</h2>
      <p style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">Match the English words with their definitions. Click two cards to flip them.</p>
      
      <div class="game-area">
        <div id="memory-grid" style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 1rem; width: 100%; max-width: 600px;"></div>
        <button class="btn" onclick="initMemoryGame()" style="margin-top: 2rem;">Restart Game</button>
        <div class="score-display" style="margin-top: 2rem;">
          <div class="score-item">
            <div class="score-value" id="memory-score">0/4</div>
            <div class="score-label">Pairs Found</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="nextSection('section-feedback')" style="margin-top: 2rem;">Finish Lesson →</button>
    </div>

    <!-- SECTION: FEEDBACK (5 min) -->
    <div class="card hidden" id="section-feedback">
      <h2 class="card-title"><span>⭐</span> Lesson Feedback</h2>
      
      <p style="margin-bottom: 2rem;">How was today's lesson? Your feedback helps us improve!</p>
      
      <div class="feedback-stars" id="stars">
        <span onclick="rate(1)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(2)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(3)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(4)">⭐</span>
        <span onclick="rate(5)">⭐</span>
      </div>
      
      <textarea id="comment" placeholder="Any comments or suggestions? What did you enjoy most?"></textarea>
      
      <button class="btn" onclick="exportFeedback()" style="margin-top: 2rem;">📥 Export Feedback JSON</button>
      
      <div class="score-display" style="margin-top: 3rem;">
        <div class="score-item">
          <div class="score-value">90 min</div>
          <div class="score-label">Completed</div>
        </div>
        <div class="score-item">
          <div class="score-value" id="final-score">0%</div>
          <div class="score-label">Overall Score</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

  </div>

  <script>
    const students = ['Nguyễn Lê Phượng Nhi','Trương Thị Quỳnh Hương','Nhật Lam','Huỳnh Gia Hãn','Khánh Duy','Gia An','Thái Vi','Trần Anh Thư','Võ Ngọc Mỹ Hoàng','Đặng Quỳnh Giang'];
    const feedback = {vocab:[],quiz:[],games:{wheel:[],reflex:0,memory:0},rating:0,comment:''};
    let quizScore = 0;
    let reflexScore = 0;
    let memoryScore = 0;
    let currentSection = 0;
    const totalSections = 7;

    function updateProgress() {
      currentSection++;
      const pct = (currentSection / totalSections) * 100;
      document.getElementById('overall-progress').style.width = pct + '%';
      document.getElementById('progress-text').textContent = Math.round(pct) + '%';
    }

    function nextSection(id) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(c => c.classList.add('hidden'));
      document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('hidden');
      updateProgress();
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }

    function playAudio(word) {
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(word);
      utterance.lang = 'en-US';
      speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
      feedback.vocab.push(word);
    }

    function answerQuiz(el, correct, qNum) {
      const siblings = el.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.quiz-option');
      siblings.forEach(s => s.style.pointerEvents = 'none');
      el.classList.add(correct ? 'quiz-correct' : 'quiz-wrong');
      if (correct) quizScore++;
      document.getElementById('quiz-score').textContent = quizScore + '/3';
      feedback.quiz.push({question: qNum, correct});
    }

    // WHEEL OF NAMES
    let wheelInterval = null;
    let wheelIndex = 0;
    function spinWheel() {
      if (wheelInterval) return;
      wheelIndex = 0;
      wheelInterval = setInterval(() => {
        wheelIndex = (wheelIndex + 1) % students.length;
        document.getElementById('wheel').textContent = students[wheelIndex];
      }, 80);
      setTimeout(() => {
        stopWheel();
        const picked = students[wheelIndex];
        document.getElementById('picked-student').textContent = '✨ ' + picked + ' ✨';
        feedback.games.wheel.push(picked);
      }, 2000 + Math.random() * 2000);
    }
    function stopWheel() {
      if (wheelInterval) {
        clearInterval(wheelInterval);
        wheelInterval = null;
      }
    }

    // REFLEX GAME
    let reflexStart = 0;
    let reflexRound = 0;
    const reflexWords = ['TRUST', 'BORED', 'TRICK', 'LESSON', 'SHEPHERD'];
    function startReflexGame() {
      reflexRound = 0;
      reflexScore = 0;
      document.getElementById('reflex-score').textContent = '0/5';
      nextReflexRound();
    }
    function nextReflexRound() {
      if (reflexRound >= 5) {
        document.getElementById('reflex-word').textContent = 'Complete!';
        document.getElementById('reflex-word').style.color = 'var(--gold)';
        return;
      }
      const word = reflexWords[reflexRound];
      document.getElementById('reflex-word').textContent = 'Get Ready...';
      document.getElementById('reflex-word').style.color = 'var(--text-dim)';
      document.getElementById('reflex-word').style.cursor = 'default';
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById('reflex-word').textContent = word;
        document.getElementById('reflex-word').style.color = '#22c55e';
        document.getElementById('reflex-word').style.cursor = 'pointer';
        document.getElementById('reflex-word').style.textShadow = '0 0 20px #22c55e';
        reflexStart = performance.now();
        
        document.getElementById('reflex-word').onclick = () => {
          if (reflexStart > 0) {
            const time = Math.round(performance.now() - reflexStart);
            document.getElementById('reflex-time').textContent = time + ' ms';
            reflexScore++;
            document.getElementById('reflex-score').textContent = reflexScore + '/5';
            reflexStart = 0;
            reflexRound++;
            feedback.games.reflex = Math.max(feedback.games.reflex, reflexScore);
            setTimeout(nextReflexRound, 1000);
          }
        };
      }, 1500 + Math.random() * 2000);
    }

    // MEMORY GAME
    const memoryPairs = [
      {word: 'Shepherd', def: 'Takes care of sheep'},
      {word: 'Bored', def: 'Feeling uninterested'},
      {word: 'Trust', def: 'Believing someone'},
      {word: 'Trick', def: 'A clever joke'}
    ];
    let memoryCards = [];
    let memoryFlipped = [];
    let memoryMatched = 0;
    
    function initMemoryGame() {
      memoryMatched = 0;
      memoryScore = 0;
      document.getElementById('memory-score').textContent = '0/4';
      memoryCards = [];
      memoryPairs.forEach((pair, i) => {
        memoryCards.push({id: i, type: 'word', content: pair.word});
        memoryCards.push({id: i, type: 'def', content: pair.def});
      });
      memoryCards.sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
      
      const grid = document.getElementById('memory-grid');
      grid.innerHTML = '';
      memoryCards.forEach((card, idx) => {
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'memory-card';
        el.style.cssText = `
          background: rgba(168,85,247,0.1);
          border: 2px solid var(--neon-purple);
          border-radius: 0.75rem;
          padding: 1rem;
          cursor: pointer;
          text-align: center;
          font-weight: 700;
          min-height: 100px;
          display: flex;
          align-items: center;
          justify-content: center;
          transition: all 0.3s;
        `;
        el.textContent = '?';
        el.onclick = () => flipCard(idx);
        grid.appendChild(el);
      });
    }
    
    function flipCard(idx) {
      if (memoryFlipped.length >= 2) return;
      const el = document.getElementById('memory-grid').children[idx];
      if (el.classList.contains('flipped') || el.classList.contains('matched')) return;
      
      el.textContent = memoryCards[idx].content;
      el.style.background = 'rgba(168,85,247,0.3)';
      el.classList.add('flipped');
      memoryFlipped.push(idx);
      
      if (memoryFlipped.length === 2) {
        setTimeout(checkMatch, 800);
      }
    }
    
    function checkMatch() {
      const [idx1, idx2] = memoryFlipped;
      const card1 = memoryCards[idx1];
      const card2 = memoryCards[idx2];
      const el1 = document.getElementById('memory-grid').children[idx1];
      const el2 = document.getElementById('memory-grid').children[idx2];
      
      if (card1.id === card2.id) {
        el1.classList.add('matched');
        el2.classList.add('matched');
        el1.style.background = 'rgba(34,197,94,0.3)';
        el2.style.background = 'rgba(34,197,94,0.3)';
        el1.style.borderColor = '#22c55e';
        el2.style.borderColor = '#22c55e';
        memoryMatched++;
        memoryScore++;
        document.getElementById('memory-score').textContent = memoryScore + '/4';
        feedback.games.memory = memoryScore;
      } else {
        el1.textContent = '?';
        el2.textContent = '?';
        el1.style.background = 'rgba(168,85,247,0.1)';
        el2.style.background = 'rgba(168,85,247,0.1)';
        el1.classList.remove('flipped');
        el2.classList.remove('flipped');
      }
      memoryFlipped = [];
    }

    // FEEDBACK
    function rate(n) {
      feedback.rating = n;
      const stars = document.getElementById('stars').children;
      Array.from(stars).forEach((star, i) => {
        star.classList.toggle('active', i < n);
      });
    }
    
    function exportFeedback() {
      feedback.comment = document.getElementById('comment').value;
      const totalScore = ((quizScore / 3) * 33 + (reflexScore / 5) * 33 + (memoryScore / 4) * 34);
      document.getElementById('final-score').textContent = Math.round(totalScore) + '%';
      
      const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(feedback, null, 2)], {type: 'application/json'});
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = 'net_lesson_feedback_' + Date.now() + '.json';
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      
      // Show success notification
      const notif = document.createElement('div');
      notif.style.cssText = `
        position: fixed;
        bottom: 2rem;
        right: 2rem;
        padding: 1rem 2rem;
        background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--gold), var(--deep-gold));
        color: black;
        font-weight: 700;
        border-radius: 0.75rem;
        box-shadow: 0 0 30px var(--gold);
        z-index: 9999;
      `;
      notif.textContent = '✅ Feedback exported successfully!';
      document.body.appendChild(notif);
      setTimeout(() => notif.remove(), 3000);
    }

    // Initialize memory game on load
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      initMemoryGame();
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
